Commissioner District is identified, the panel will display information for that district's Commissioner.</p><p style=\"text-align: left;\"><br></p><p style=\"text-align: left;\">Information may include:</p><ol><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Contact information</li><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Commissioner history and background</li><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Boards and committees</li><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Liaison assignments</li><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Other Commissioner information</li></ol><p style=\"text-align: left;\"><br></p><p style=\"text-align: left;\">There are individual information views for Districts 1 through 5.</p><p style=\"text-align: left;\"><br></p><p style=\"text-align: left;\"><strong>Browsing Commissioner Information </strong></p><p style=\"text-align: left;\"><br></p><p style=\"text-align: left;\">Use the left and right arrows on either side of the bottom panel to move between the different Commissioner District information pages.</p><p style=\"text-align: left;\"><br></p><p style=\"text-align: left;\"><strong>District Demographics</strong></p><p style=\"text-align: left;\"><br></p><p style=\"text-align: left;\">Each Commissioner District includes a District at a Glance demographic profile to provide residents with additional information about the communities represented within the district.</p><p style=\"text-align: left;\"><br></p><p style=\"text-align: left;\">Statistics include:</p><ol><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Population</li><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Households</li><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Median age</li><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Median household income</li><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Median home value</li><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Homeownership</li><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Population age 65 and older</li><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Veteran population</li><li data-list=\"bullet\" style=\"text-align: left;\"><span class=\"ql-ui\" contenteditable=\"false\"></span>Educational attainment</li></ol><p style=\"text-align: left;\"><br></p><p style=\"text-align: left;\">These statistics were created using ArcGIS GeoEnrichment, which estimates demographic characteristics for geographic areas such as Commissioner District boundaries. Because Commissioner Districts do not necessarily follow Census geography boundaries, GeoEnrichment uses data-allocation methods to estimate values for each district. Esri's GeoEnrichment service uses the best available apportionment method to assign demographic information to custom geographic
